On the official benchmark, evaluated using the Erasing-Retention-Robustness (ERR) metric on Stable Diffusion v1.4, our method outperforms the state-of-the-art baseline by 12.1\% on average across the five concept categories, achieving substantial gains.

</details>


### [83] [DreamX-Phi 1.0: Action-Conditioned Video World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13489)
*DreamX Team,Rui Chen,Xiangxiang Chu,Geng Li,Jifan Li,Qingfeng Shi,Datao Tang,Jing Tang,Jun Wang,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DreamX-Phi 1.0，一个用于机器人操作的动作条件视频世界模型，在WorldArena 2.0挑战赛中取得Track 1第一名和Track 2第二名的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动作条件视频世界模型虽然能够生成逼真的视频，但逼真度不等于忠实度——生成的rollout可能移动错误的机械臂或丢失被操作物体，无法保证预测严格遵循每个机械臂的指定路径规划。

Method: 主要包括四个关键组件：(1) 通过PRoPE风格几何编码将每只机械臂的SE(3)变换注入注意力机制，保留机械臂身份和刚体运动结构；(2) 引入轻量级深度分支以约束场景级几何信息；(3) 使用SAM3掩码配合冻结的V-JEPA教师模型来维持抓取过程中小物体的对象一致性；(4) 通过分布匹配蒸馏将多步生成器蒸馏为少步学生模型以实现高效部署。

Result: 在WorldArena 2.0挑战赛中，DreamX-Phi 1.0取得Track 1第一名和Track 2第二名，代码和模型将公开。

Conclusion: 通过几何感知的动作条件注入与对象一致性维持机制，显著提升了操作视频世界模型对动作指令的忠实度，同时通过蒸馏实现高效推理，证明了复杂动作遵循与几何约束可以兼顾。

Abstract: We present \textbf{DreamX-Phi 1.0}, an action-conditioned video world model for robotic manipulation that, given an observed frame, a language instruction, and a prescribed action sequence comprising end-effector poses and gripper states, predicts the resulting future observations. Yet realism alone does not guarantee faithfulness: a convincing rollout can still move the wrong arm or lose the manipulated object. To ensure the prediction respects each arm's commanded path, we inject per-arm $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ transformations into attention via \textbf{PRoPE-style geometric encoding}, preserving arm identity and rigid-motion structure. Action control alone does not fully constrain scene geometry or the evolution of small manipulated objects. We therefore add a lightweight \textbf{depth branch} for scene-level geometry and use \textbf{SAM3 masks} with a frozen \textbf{V-JEPA teacher} to maintain object consistency throughout grasping. We further distill the multi-step generator into a few-step student via distribution-matching distillation for efficient deployment. At the time of writing, \model{} achieves first place on Track~1 and second place on Track~2 of the WorldArena~2.0 Challenge. Our model and code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [84] [TraVEL: Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning for Driving-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13495)
*Yi-Chung Chen,Philip Jacobson,Tom Lampo,Yiren Lu,Jin Yao,David I. Inouye,Jing Gao,Danhua Guo,Burhan Yaman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TraVEL（轨迹引导的视频嵌入学习）框架，通过结合监督微调（SFT）和基于自我轨迹相似度的组相对策略优化（GRPO）奖励，将通用多模态嵌入模型Qwen3-VL-Embedding适配为驾驶视频检索任务。该方法利用nuReasoning中的成对片段和推理轨迹数据进行训练，在推理时仅依赖单向量视频嵌入即可完成检索，无需专家规则或辅助感知输出。在自建的驾驶视频检索基准上，TraVEL在2B和8B模型规模上分别将纵向和横向mAP提升了9.8/4.7和7.2/1.5个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶日志的大规模检索对数据整理、模型开发和安全性分析至关重要，但现有方法存在两条路线各自的缺陷：结构化规则检索需要专家规则、辅助数据和多阶段感知管线，系统复杂；通用多模态嵌入模型虽简单高效，却依赖静态场景上下文捷径，难以区分左转/右转、加速/减速等运动中心事件。因此需要一种能兼顾效率与精细运动理解的方法。

Method: 两阶段训练流程：(1) 先在nuReasoning数据集上用InfoNCE目标对Qwen3-VL-Embedding进行成对片段与推理轨迹的监督微调（SFT）；(2) 引入TraVEL框架，将自我轨迹相似度作为奖励信号，通过Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）进行运动感知的强化微调。轨迹仅作为特权训练监督，推理时仍使用单向量视频嵌入。同时基于nuReasoning构建了驾驶视频检索基准。

Result: 在两个模型规模上均显著提升运动中心检索性能：相对于SFT基线，2B模型上纵向和横向mAP分别提升9.8和4.7个点；8B模型上分别提升7.2和1.5个点。轨迹监督在跨规模上均有效，且推理时无需任何额外信息。

Conclusion: TraVEL将物理有据的轨迹监督与高效的基于嵌入的检索相结合，有效弥补了纯文本监督在细粒度运动理解上的不足，为驾驶视频检索提供了一个兼顾精确性和推理效率的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficiently retrieving relevant clips from large-scale driving logs is essential for data curation, model development, and safety analysis. Structured and rule-based retrieval systems can explicitly target driving events, but typically require expert-defined rules, auxiliary data, and multi-stage perception pipelines. Multimodal embedding models offer a simpler and more efficient alternative by representing each video with a single searchable vector. However, general-purpose models often rely on shortcuts from static scene context and struggle to distinguish motion-centric events, such as turning left versus right or accelerating versus decelerating. In this work, we study how to adapt a general-purpose multimodal embedding model to driving-video retrieval. We first fine-tune Qwen3-VL-Embedding on paired clips and reasoning traces from nuReasoning using an InfoNCE objective. While this stage substantially improves overall retrieval, caption supervision alone remains insufficient for fine-grained motion understanding. We therefore introduce TraVEL (Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning), a motion-aware fine-tuning framework that uses ego-trajectory similarity as a reward within Group Relative Policy Optimization. Trajectories serve only as privileged training supervision; retrieval still operates on single-vector video embeddings without ego poses, expert rules, or auxiliary perception outputs. We further construct a driving-video retrieval benchmark from nuReasoning. Experiments show that TraVEL improves motion-centric retrieval across model scales: relative to SFT, it raises longitudinal and lateral mAP by 9.8 and 4.7 points at 2B, with corresponding gains of 7.2 and 1.5 points at 8B. TraVEL thus combines physically grounded supervision with efficient embedding-based search.

</details>


### [85] [GS$^{2}$CI: Robust Gaussian Splatting For Snapshot Compressive Imaging via Large Vision Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13502)
*Yanming Yang,Chenxi Song,Ping Wang,Xin Yuan,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从单一快照压缩成像（SCI）测量中重建高质量3D场景的框架，结合3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）与大规模视觉基础模型（VFMs）的先验，并引入SCI特定的稠密化策略OSGR，在多个基准上取得领先的重建质量与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快照压缩成像（SCI）是一种高效的高速视频/多视角场景采集方案，但现有基于SCI的3D场景重建方法面临信息丢失严重、视角多样性有限、以及联合优化3D表示与相机位姿计算负担大等核心挑战，亟需一种能克服上述问题的重建框架。

Method: 方法包含三个关键部分：（1）主重建阶段：利用测量衍生的3D视觉基础模型（VFM）初始化3DGS表示，并采用SCI感知的高斯优化；（2）粗阶段收敛后，辅助2D VFM在合成视角提供伪视图监督，用于局部外观细化；（3）引入OSGR（Opacity-Guided Splitting and Growth Regulation）策略，通过局部不透明度统计增强分裂候选、均值不透明度正则化抑制损失补偿性的不透明度膨胀，并以候选比例与高斯数量约束限制表示增长，解决SCI监督模糊导致的不稳定性。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，该方法取得综合最优性能，在重建质量和对视角变化的鲁棒性方面均领先，同时保持有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将3DGS与大规模视觉基础模型先验相结合、并辅以SCI特定的稠密化正则化策略，能够有效解决从单张SCI测量中重建高质量3D场景这一难题，为SCI领域提供了新的方法论路径。

Abstract: Snapshot Compressive Imaging (SCI) offers an efficient solution for high-speed video acquisition and, under exposure-time camera--scene relative motion, multi-view scene capture by compressing temporal or spatial information into a single 2D measurement. While recent studies have explored SCI for 3D scene reconstruction, existing methods struggle with significant challenges due to information loss, limited viewpoint diversity, and the computational burden of jointly optimizing 3D representations and camera poses. In this work, we propose a novel framework that reconstructs high-quality 3D scenes from a single SCI measurement by leveraging 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and the powerful priors of large-scale vision foundation models (VFMs). Our primary reconstruction combines measurement-derived 3D VFM initialization with SCI-aware Gaussian optimization. After coarse-stage convergence, an auxiliary 2D VFM provides pseudo-view supervision at synthesized viewpoints for local appearance refinement. To further address the instability caused by ambiguous SCI supervision during 3DGS optimization, we introduce Opacity-Guided Splitting and Growth Regulation (OSGR), an SCI-specific densification strategy that augments split candidates using local opacity statistics, discourages loss-compensating opacity inflation through mean-opacity regulation, and bounds representation growth with explicit candidate-ratio and Gaussian-count constraints. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves the strongest overall performance, combining leading reconstruction quality and robustness to viewpoint variation with competitive computational efficiency.

</details>


### [86] [TabSOM: A tabular-to-image encoding method based on self-organizing maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13513)
*David Chushig-Muzo,María Ángeles Rodríguez de Cara,Eva Milara,Francisco J. Lara-Abelenda,Luis Zhinin-Vera,Diego H. Peluffo-Ordóñez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TabSOM，一种基于自组织映射（SOM）的表格数据到图像编码方法，通过匈牙利分配实现特征空间的固定布局，并在图像中堆叠多尺度节点通道以同时编码特征值及特征间交互关系，在十二种基线方法对比中排名第一或第二，且提供两种可解释性工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格到图像方法（如t-SNE、UMAP、PCA映射）仅编码每个特征的单变量边际值，丢弃了特征间的关系信息，导致转换后的图像表征信息不完整，限制了CNN和视觉Transformer在表格数据上的性能上限。

Method: TabSOM的核心包括：(1) 利用SOM的组件平面（component planes），通过无碰撞匈牙利分配为每个输入特征分配固定的画布位置；(2) 构建捕获成对特征关系的图结构；(3) 生成堆叠图像，包含两个多尺度节点通道——一个编码特征值（固定尺度），另一个编码特征间交互（作为相关特征之间的空间连接）；(4) 提出两种基于SOM的可解释性方法：原型启发的部分依赖图和类别分离重要性评分。

Result: 在十二种现有表格到图像方法的对比中，TabSOM在每个公开二分类数据集上排名第一或第二，并且是评估的所有方法中方差最低的。可解释性结果与随机森林、XGBoost和SHAP对比验证，类别分离评分在Top特征上与既有基线合理一致，同时捕获了输入数据中的互补结构信息。

Conclusion: TabSOM为将深度学习架构应用于表格数据提供了一种有效且可解释的方案，弥合了该领域性能与可解释性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Tabular-to-image methods have emerged as novel approaches to leverage the high predictive performance of convolutional neural networks and vision transformers. They convert tabular data into image representations, mapping each feature at a fixed pixel location derived from a dimensionality-reduction method (e.g., t-SNE, UMAP, PCA). However, they encode only the marginal value of each feature and discard information about feature relationships. We propose TabSOM, a tabular-to-image encoding built on the Self-Organizing Map (SOM), which provides: (i) a spatial layout in which every input feature occupies a fixed canvas position derived from its component plane via collision-free Hungarian assignment; and (ii) a graph that captures pairwise feature relationships derived from the SOM component planes. The resulting image stacks two multi-scale node channels: one encodes feature values at fixed scales, while the other encodes pairwise feature interactions as spatial connections between related features. Two SOM-derived interpretability approaches are introduced: a prototype-inspired partial dependence plot and a class--separation importance score. Benchmarked against twelve existing tabular-to-image methods across public binary-classification datasets, TabSOM ranks first or second on every dataset and achieves the lowest variance of any method evaluated. Interpretability obtained with TabSOM was validated against Random Forest, XGBoost, and SHAP, the class-separation score shows reasonable agreement with established baselines on the top-ranked features while capturing complementary structural information from input data. These results demonstrate that TabSOM provides an effective and interpretable approach for applying deep learning architectures to tabular data, bridging the performance--interpretability gap in this domain.

</details>


### [87] [SCULPT: Subtractive Composition for 3D Part Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13541)
*Sikuang Li,Chen Yang,Jiemin Fang,Jiazhong Cen,Yuhe Wei,Jichen Pang,Wei Shen,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCULPT框架，通过减法组合（subtractive composition）实现部件感知的3D生成。不同于现有方法在生成后分割或通过加法拼接部件，SCULPT在结构化3D潜空间中迭代地使用联合分裂预测器，同时生成提取部件和剩余物体，使部件与剩余部分联合生成而非事后拼接。实验在PartObjaverse上取得SOTA几何质量，并保持组装后的完整物体重建能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部件感知3D生成方法存在根本性缺陷：基于分割的方法在部件边界确定之前就已固定物体的几何形状，导致部件语义与真实生成过程脱节；基于加法的方法虽然能显式暴露部件数量，但共享边界容易出现间隙、穿插和材质不连续等问题。需要一个既能保持完整物体连贯性、又能自然暴露结构部件的生成框架。

Method: SCULPT采用减法组合策略：(1) 在结构化3D潜空间中表示完整物体；(2) 迭代应用联合分裂预测器，每次生成一个提取部件和剩余物体；(3) 预测器执行耦合去噪过程，同时以图像和当前3D状态为条件；(4) 在原生稀疏3D支撑的并集上处理两个输出，允许支撑重叠而非强制不相交分割；(5) 当剩余支撑为空或达到安全上限时终止展开，使部件数量自适应于每个物体。

Result: 在PartObjaverse基准上取得SOTA几何质量，同时保持部件组装后的强完整物体重建。在四个数据集图像、一个文本到图像生成输入和一个真实世界照片上展示出细粒度的纹理部件分解，超越了基准测试范围。

Conclusion: 减法组合是部件感知3D生成的一种有效范式，通过将部件提取与剩余物体生成耦合在去噪过程中，既保留了完整物体的几何连贯性，又实现了自适应的、无拼接伪影的部件分解，且能泛化到生成图像和真实照片等基准之外的数据。

Abstract: Part-aware 3D generation aims to create digital assets that are coherent as complete objects while exposing structural parts for editing, material assignment, animation, and reuse. Existing methods impose this structure outside the native generation loop: segmentation-based methods partition an already generated shape, while additive methods synthesize parts from predefined layouts, boxes, or tokens and then reconcile them into a whole. The former preserves the generated geometry but fixes the object before part boundaries are determined; the latter exposes part cardinality but often leaves shared boundaries vulnerable to gaps, interpenetrations, and material discontinuities. In this paper, we propose SCULPT, a framework that addresses these challenges through subtractive composition. Given a complete object represented in a structured 3D latent space, SCULPT iteratively applies a joint split predictor to generate one extracted part together with the remaining object. The predictor performs a coupled denoising process conditioned on both the image and the current 3D state, so the extracted part and updated remainder are generated together rather than reconciled after generation. The joint split predictor processes both outputs on the union of their native sparse 3D supports, allowing neighboring supports to overlap rather than imposing a disjoint voxel partition. The rollout ends when the remainder support becomes empty or reaches a fixed safety cap, allowing the number of generated parts to adapt to each object within that bound. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art geometry on PartObjaverse while preserving strong complete-object reconstruction after part assembly. Results on four dataset images, one text-to-image-generated input, and one real-world photograph further show fine-grained textured part decomposition beyond the benchmark.

</details>


### [88] [Alaya-EVOKE: From Linear-Scaling Supervision to Endless World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13546)
*Yuanyang Yin,Gongxuan Wang,Yifan Zhan,Chuanhao Li,Kaipeng Zhang,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Evoke是一种交互式世界模型，通过外部化持久世界状态（相机索引的世界状态库）和重新设计教师模型（稀疏注意力+分块分组+远程帧检索+线性注意力全局状态），在有限上下文和循环外部记忆中支持开放式的长时程生成。其30秒分布匹配目标通过自强制rollout将能力蒸馏到三步学生模型中（无分类器引导），在单块H200上每秒1.5秒chunk生成耗时2.11秒，在WBench上达到SOTA，在VBench-Long和VBench-2.0上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型需要同时满足持久记忆、响应式交互和长时程生成三个要求，但这些要求相互冲突：在denoiser上下文或KV缓存中维护历史会导致成本随会话增长而增加，迫使在会话长度和保留记忆之间权衡；而低延迟交互依赖少步生成，其能力受限于教师模型。

Method: Evoke通过两个方面解决这些局限：1）将场景几何外部化到相机索引的世界状态库中，仅检索视图相关信息，使denoiser上下文在会话增长时保持有界；2）将教师模型重新设计为长时程监督的生成器，其稀疏注意力结合了分块分组、选定远帧的检索和线性注意力全局状态，使记忆和计算线性增长。还引入了30秒分布匹配目标，在自强制rollout下将能力蒸馏到不使用分类器引导的三步学生模型。

Result: Evoke支持开放式、连续演化的生成；在单块H200、384×640分辨率下，每1.5秒chunk生成耗时2.11秒。作为三步世界模型，在WBench上达到SOTA性能，在VBench-Long和VBench-2.0上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: Evoke通过有界的上下文和循环外部记忆，解决了交互式世界模型在持久记忆与低延迟生成之间的根本矛盾，证明了外部世界状态库+长时程教师蒸馏的方案能够同时实现开放式的长期生成和响应式交互控制。

Abstract: Interactive world models must support persistent memory, responsive interaction, and long-horizon generation, yet these requirements place conflicting demands on the model. Maintaining history in the denoiser context or key-value cache incurs growing cost, forcing a trade-off between session length and retained memory, while low-latency interaction relies on few-step generation whose capabilities are bounded by its teacher. Evoke addresses both limitations by externalizing persistent world state and redesigning the teacher for long-horizon interactive generation. Scene geometry is maintained in an external, camera-indexed world state bank, from which only view-relevant information is retrieved, keeping the denoiser context bounded as the session grows. Rather than treating the teacher as a fixed generator, we design it for long-horizon supervision: its sparse attention combines chunk-wise grouping, retrieval of selected distant frames, and a linear-attention global state, yielding linear growth in memory and compute while enabling supervision over long horizons. Such supervision exposes content drift that stays locally plausible within short windows, while per-chunk conditioning enables prompt changes and event control throughout the sequence. A 30-second distribution-matching objective, applied under self-forced rollouts, transfers both capabilities to a three-step student that uses no classifier-free guidance, improving resistance to long-term drift while preserving responsive conditioning. With bounded context and recurrent external memory, Evoke supports open-ended, continuously evolving generation; on a single H200 at $384\times 640$, each $1.5\,\mathrm{s}$ chunk is generated in $2.11\,\mathrm{s}$. As a three-step world model, Evoke achieves state-of-the-art performance on WBench while remaining competitive on VBench-Long and VBench-2.0.

</details>


### [89] [PlayWorld: Benchmarking World Models with Agent Players over Long-Horizon Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13552)
*Kaixin Ding,Xi Chen,Minghong Cai,Zhiyuan Xu,Yiyang Wang,Yuxiang Lu,Junyi Li,Shuyang Chen,Yuan Gao,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了PlayWorld基准，利用多模态Agent Players与视频世界模型交互，以评估模型在长视界目标下的表现，涵盖171个场景和四个核心维度（几何一致性、交互保真度、视野外演化、洞察演化）。实验表明现有世界模型在长视界交互目标上仍不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在视频一致性和行动可控性上表现优异，但公平比较这些交互式模型具有挑战性。固定的基于动作序列评估不适合跨模型比较，因为不同模型为达成同一目标所需的行为序列可能有很大差异，需要更符合人类真实评估方式的交互范式。

Method: 采用多模态Agent Players与世界模型交互以达成指定的长视界目标，构建PlayWorld基准，包含171个场景和明确目标。评估涵盖四个核心维度：几何一致性、交互保真度、视野外演化和洞察演化，同时加入视频质量和可控性的基础能力指标。

Result: 在九个最先进的世界模型上进行实验，发现当前模型在长视界交互目标上仍不可靠，尤其在维持空间一致性和持久状态演化方面表现欠佳。

Conclusion: 基于Agent交互的评估范式比固定动作条件评估更能公平比较世界模型，且当前世界模型在长视界交互中仍有显著提升空间，特别是在空间一致性和状态演化方面。

Abstract: Video world models simulate future states conditioned on current observations and user actions. Recent systems have demonstrated impressive video consistency and action controllability over long sequences. However, fairly comparing these interactive models remains challenging. In practice, a human player typically evaluates a world model by pursuing long-horizon objectives through interaction. For example, a user may turn around 360 degrees to see whether the environment remains consistent, or walk into the water and inspect whether realistic water ripples are generated. The action sequence required to achieve the same objective may vary substantially between models, making fixed action-conditioned evaluation unsuitable for cross-model comparison. To address this, we employ multi-modal Agent Players to interact with world models toward specified long-horizon objectives. Building on this paradigm, we introduce PlayWorld, a benchmark providing 171 scenarios, each with a specified objective. To evaluate performance thoroughly, we assess models along four core dimensions: geometry consistency, interaction fidelity, out-of-sight evolution, and insight evolution. In addition, we incorporate basic ability metrics for video quality and controllability. Experiments across nine state-of-the-art world models reveal that current models remain unreliable on long-horizon interactive objectives, particularly in maintaining spatial consistency and persistent state evolution. Code and data are available at https://github.com/kxding/PlayWorld.

</details>


### [90] [V-RAE: Rethinking Video Latent Spaces for Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13556)
*Minghui Guo,Shengqiong Wu,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 V-RAE（视频表示自编码器），在冻结的视觉基础模型表示之上构建紧凑的生成式潜在空间，通过轻量级时间池化模块去除时间冗余并保留语义结构，实验表明其在视频重建、语义探测和类别条件生成等任务上均优于现有大规模预训练视频VAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频自编码器的潜在空间主要针对像素级重建进行优化，缺乏高层语义组织，而重建最优的潜在空间并不一定适合生成建模。本文旨在构建兼具重建能力与语义组织能力的视频潜在空间，以更好支撑下游生成任务。

Method: V-RAE 采用冻结的视觉基础模型编码器提取语义特征，通过一个轻量级时间池化模块去除时间冗余并保持语义结构，再由视频解码器从压缩特征中重建连续运动。作者在四种冻结编码器上评估了该框架，并提出了 tFVD 这一时间一致性诊断指标，以更可靠地关联下游生成质量。

Result: V-RAE 在 K600 上取得 2.13 rFVD，优于所有被评估的大规模预训练视频VAE；其潜在空间保留的语义信息显著多于传统视频分词器。在匹配的生成设置下，最优变体在 UCF101 和 K600 上分别取得 gFVD 117.86 和 19.16，收敛速度快达6倍。此外，V-RAE 在 Cityscapes 上的未来视频预测任务中也优于 Wan 2.2 VAE 潜在空间。

Conclusion: 实验表明，冻结的语义表示能够同时支持视频重建、生成和预测建模；仅凭重建质量不足以刻画生成效用，tFVD 作为时间一致性诊断指标能更可靠地预测下游生成性能。

Abstract: Latent video generation relies on autoencoders to define a compact space in which generative models operate. Although video autoencoder architectures have evolved substantially, their latent spaces are still optimized primarily for pixel-level reconstruction and provide limited high-level semantic organization. A reconstruction-optimal latent space, however, need not be well suited to generative modeling. We propose V-RAE, a video representation autoencoder that builds compact generative latents on top of frozen vision foundation model representations. A lightweight temporal pooling module removes temporal redundancy while preserving semantic structure, and a video decoder reconstructs continuous motion from the compressed features. We evaluate V-RAE with four representative frozen encoders on video reconstruction, semantic probing, and class-conditional generation. V-RAE achieves 2.13 rFVD on K600, outperforming all evaluated large-scale pretrained video VAEs. Its latents retain substantially more semantic information than conventional video tokenizer latents. Under matched generation settings, our best variant achieves gFVD scores of 117.86 and 19.16 on UCF101 and K600, respectively, while converging up to 6x faster}. We further show that reconstruction quality alone is insufficient to characterize generative utility and introduce tFVD, a temporal-coherence diagnostic that correlates more reliably with downstream generation quality. Beyond video generation, V-RAE also improves future video prediction on Cityscapes over the Wan 2.2 VAE latent space under matched prediction settings. Taken together, the experiments show that frozen semantic representations can support video reconstruction, generation, and predictive modeling. The project page: https://v-rae.github.io/.

</details>


### [91] [AutoDesign: Meta-Harness Optimization for Long-Horizon Agentic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13560)
*Yaxin Luo,Haobin Jiang,Jialv Zou,Xu Huang,Wenhao Yan,Haodong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Jing Li,Xiaofu Chen,Xiaohan Zhao,Jiacheng Liu,Jiacheng Cui,Zhiqiang Shen,Xiaotong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoDesign是一个将多模态源转化为结构化媒体输出的长期智能体框架，通过元优化器引导代码智能体基于回滚反馈递归改进harness系统，在论文到海报生成任务上超越商业系统Claude Design 7.45分，并在7种受控配置下平均提升12.4%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想的harness系统应与人设计先验对齐，并通过经验探索积累可复用经验以实现递归自改进，但现有范式是静态的，缺乏这种能力。

Method: 提出AutoDesign框架，包含一个元harness优化器，引导代码智能体基于回滚（rollout）反馈递归地改进harness系统。同时构建PosterBench基准（100篇论文主轨+10篇子集），在学术论文到海报生成任务上进行实例化与评估。

Result: 在主轨上取得78.32分，超越Claude Design 7.45分；在7种受控配置下集成学习到的DesignHarness将平均得分从54.99提升至67.39（+12.4%）；全自主长期循环中40分钟执行253次工具调用和11次编辑迭代，成本低于3美元，达到平均会议海报质量；系统盲人研究显示最高人类偏好。

Conclusion: AutoDesign验证了元harness优化驱动的递归自改进范式在长期智能体媒体生成任务中的有效性，在性能、效率和人类偏好上均优于现有静态系统。

Abstract: Transforming multimodal sources into condensed and structured media outputs can be fundamentally conceptualized as a long-horizon agentic process centered on a model-harness system. While an ideal harness system should align with human design priors and accumulate reusable experience through empirical exploration to drive recursive self-improvement, existing paradigms remain static and fall short of this capability. In this paper, we present AutoDesign, a framework that aligns with human design priors, where a meta-harness optimizer guides a code agent to recursively improve harness based on rollout feedback. To instantiate and evaluate this framework, we focus on the academic paper-to-poster generation task and introduce PosterBench, comprising a 100-paper Main Track spanning five disciplines and PosterBench-mini, a shared 10-paper subset for controlled evaluation. On the PosterBench Main Track, AutoDesign achieves the highest score of 78.32, surpassing the closed-source commercial system Claude Design by 7.45 points. Across seven controlled code-agent-model configurations, integrating the learned DesignHarness consistently improves performance, increasing the average PosterBench Score from 54.99 to 67.39 (+12.4%). In a fully autonomous long-horizon loop, it executes 253 tool calls and 11 editing turns within 40 minutes for under $3, reaching average conference-poster quality in human evaluation. A system-blind human study further demonstrates that AutoDesign achieves the highest human preference among evaluated systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [LoKiFormer: Locality-aware Attention with Decoupled Knowledge Memory for Efficient Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12419)
*Qiuwu Chen,Zimo Liu,Yuchen Li,Ying Sun,Yifan Zhang,Zhijie Qiu,Zeng You,Ryan Dong,Simeng Ma,Yaofo Chen,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoKiFormer是一种新型LLM架构，通过局部融合注意力（LFA）显式捕捉局部模式，并通过知识记忆模块（KMM）将全局知识存储与计算路径解耦，在预训练中比基线模型收敛快1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM架构在预训练中存在两大低效问题：(1)自注意力缺乏对局部性的显式归纳偏置，导致序列内部局部信息被冗余建模；(2)混合专家（MoE）将知识存储与计算路径隐式耦合，阻碍了对序列外部全局知识的灵活访问。

Method: 在标准解码器上新增两个专用模块：1) 局部融合注意力（LFA），在注意力中引入卷积融合，显式捕获局部模式并使注意力作用于更具信息量的表示；2) 知识记忆模块（KMM），引入参数化键值记忆，将全局知识显式存储在可寻址的槽位中，实现存储与计算解耦并支持直接知识检索。

Result: 实验结果表明，LoKiFormer在预训练中比基线模型收敛速度快1.33倍，证明了其相对现有LLM架构的优越性。

Conclusion: LFA和KMM两个模块共同使LoKiFormer在局部和全局两个信息层级上实现更高效、更有效的信息整合，从而提升预训练效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable breakthroughs across various applications. However, their architectures remain inefficient in pretraining due to two main limitations: (i) self-attention lacks an explicit inductive bias for locality, leading to redundant modeling of sequence-internal local information; (ii) mixture-of-experts (MoE) implicitly couples knowledge storage with computational pathways, hindering flexible access to sequence-external global knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we propose LoKiFormer, a novel LLM architecture that augments the standard decoder with two dedicated modules: 1) Local Fusion Attention (LFA), which incorporates a convolutional fusion to attention, explicitly capturing local patterns and allowing the attention to operate on more informative representations; 2) Knowledge Memory Module (KMM), which introduces a parametric key-value memory that explicitly stores global knowledge in addressable slots, decoupling storage from computation and enabling direct knowledge retrieval. Together, these modules enable LoKiFormer to achieve more efficient and effective integration of information at both levels. Experimental results show that LoKiFormer converges 1.33x faster in pre-training than baseline models, underscoring its superiority over existing LLM architectures.

</details>


### [93] [Which Site, and When: A Free-Satellite-Data Test of Himalayan Glacial Lake Bursts, Landslides, and Ice Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12422)
*Matthew Kahn,Milan Arjel,Nirmala Adhikari,Mingmar Sherpa,James Pope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究基于免费卫星数据（雷达干涉测量与卫星天气），对尼泊尔喜马拉雅地区的三种相关灾害（冰碛/冰坝湖溃决、降雨触发滑坡、冰川小池塘洪水）建立预测模型。利用589次已记录的溃决事件和数千次滑坡，通过空间交叉验证评估模型性能：前兆天气在触发时间预测上ROC达0.73-0.83，地形在易损性排序上的真实表现仅为0.54-0.76（部分接近随机水平）。深度学习方法未能显著超越梯度提升基线模型，最终提供一份尼泊尔优先排序清单作为辅助工具而非精确预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期可行性研究已证明卫星雷达干涉仪可识别冰碛坝变形、卫星气象数据可标记湖泊受压时期，但未提出预测模型。本文旨在填补这一空白，用免费数据构建可预测"哪些地点易发"和"何时触发"的模型，为高危冰湖溃决预警提供依据。

Method: 使用589起有年代的冰湖溃决事件（HMAGLOFDB）和数千条滑坡记录，为每个事件匹配相似但未失稳的地点作为对照。采用空间交叉验证（整块地图瓦片留出法）防止邻域信息泄漏。分别评估前兆天气（触发时序）和地形特征（易损性排序），并比较五种深度学习模型与梯度提升基线模型的表现，同时用三规则决策树（基于崎岖度和季风降雨）复现基线。<br>三种灾害分别独立建模：大型冰碛/冰坝溃决、降雨触发滑坡、冰川周边小池塘洪水。

Result: 前兆天气预测触发时间的ROC为：大型溃决0.73、滑坡0.83、小洪水0.82。地形排序在朴素评分下看似接近0.9，但匹配对照地点后的真实值为0.76（溃决）、0.71（滑坡）、0.54（小洪水，等同随机）。溃决信号在单一区域内保持稳健，尼泊尔单独ROC达0.89。深度学习模型未决定性超越梯度提升基线：仅滑坡上略高约3个百分点，证据不足；对湖泊灾害基线模型直接胜出。

Conclusion: 免费卫星数据足以对喜马拉雅冰湖溃决风险提供有价值的排序与触发预警，但存在明确的能力边界：小池塘洪水的地形易损性预测无法超越随机水平，深度学习在此类小样本任务中不具备优势。最终交付尼泊尔风险优先排序清单，明确其定位为优先级辅助工具而非精确预测。

Abstract: Two free satellite signals carry real information about glacial-lake outburst risk in the Nepal Himalaya: radar interferometry sees a moraine dam slowly sagging, and satellite weather marks the weeks when a primed lake is under stress. A companion feasibility study found that deformation indicates which lake is destabilizing and weather indicates when it is at risk, but proposed no predictive model. To address this gap, we propose and evaluate models that predict which site is susceptible and when a trigger arrives. We test three related hazards on free data alone: large moraine- and ice-dammed bursts, rainfall-triggered landslides, and smaller floods from ponds on and around a glacier. Each hazard gets two questions, never blended. Using 589 dated outbursts from HMAGLOFDB and several thousand catalogued landslides, we match each event against similar but unfailed sites, and hold every model to a strong simple baseline under spatial cross-validation that withholds whole map tiles, so no model succeeds by recognising a trained-on neighbourhood. Antecedent weather times the trigger at ROC 0.73 for big bursts, 0.83 for landslides, and 0.82 for small floods. Terrain ranks susceptibility only in part: scored naively it appears near 0.9, largely because catalogued failures cluster in wetter ranges; matched against comparable nearby sites the honest figures are 0.76, 0.71, and 0.54 (no better than chance). The burst signal holds within single regions, reaching 0.89 in Nepal alone. Five deep-learning models do not decisively beat a simple gradient-boosted baseline. Three score marginally higher on landslides, a hint too small to confirm. For the lake hazards the baseline wins outright, reproduced by a three-rule decision tree on ruggedness and monsoon rainfall. We close with a ranked Nepal watchlist, a prioritisation aid, not a prediction, and note where free data reaches its limits.

</details>


### [94] [MARCH: Scaling Recurrent Memory with Content-Routed State Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12435)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Chaochao Lu,Youbang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MARCH架构，通过周期性地将循环状态快照缓存为"状态锚点"并结合内容条件化的锚键，使状态空间模型能够在保持高效解码的同时扩展记忆容量，在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上超越多种线性注意力变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer虽然具备随上下文长度增长的长上下文检索能力，但训练时计算复杂度为二次方，推理时KV缓存线性增长；而循环替代模型虽然解码高效，但固定尺寸的状态压缩会覆盖早期信息，导致在召回密集型任务上表现不佳。如何在两者之间取得平衡是一个关键挑战。

Method: MARCH架构的核心是周期性缓存累计的循环状态检查点作为"状态锚点"，并为每个锚点关联紧凑的内容条件化锚键。模型维护一个可随上下文长度增长的记忆库，在每个token处生成锚查询以注意力方式聚合所有因果可用的历史锚点，从而在不改变原生计算路径的前提下实现内容路由的状态缓存。

Result: 经过标准预训练后，MARCH在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上持续优于多种线性注意力变体。

Conclusion: 内容路由的状态缓存能够显著增强循环模型的长距离记忆能力，同时保持其原生高效的解码路径，为长序列建模提供了可控的历史分辨率与记忆成本权衡方案。

Abstract: Transformers owe much of their strong long-context retrieval capability to a token-level memory that grows with context length. This flexibility, however, incurs a quadratic computation complexity during training and a key--value cache that grows linearly during autoregressive inference. Recurrent alternatives offer efficient decoding by compressing the entire history into a fixed-size state, but often underperform on recall-intensive tasks since earlier associations usually get overwritten by subsequent updates, and only the most recent contextual information is retained. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that effectively scales state-space models beyond a fixed-size dimension, while maintaining computational efficiency over long-sequences. MARCH periodically caches cumulative recurrent-state checkpoints as state anchors and associates each anchor with a compact, content-conditioned anchor key. This lets MARCH maintain a memory bank, which can grow as context length increases, providing a controllable trade-off between historical resolution and memory cost. At each token, MARCH produces an anchor query to attend all causally available state anchors, and the output is calculated as an attention-style aggregation over all historical anchors along the current state. We show that after standard pretraining, MARCH consistently outperforms multiple linear attention variants across commonsense reasoning, LongBench, and in-context retrieval. These results demonstrate that content-routed state caching substantially strengthens recurrent long-range memory while preserving its native computation path.

</details>


### [95] [Multi-AUV Ad-hoc network-based Target Tracking: A Value Gradient Guidance Multi-Agent Diffusion Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12436)
*Jiaao Ma,Chuan Lin,Guangjie Han,Shengchao Zhu,Qian Zhu,Ying Liu,Zhenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VGG-MADiffRL（值梯度引导的多智能体扩散强化学习算法）和MDCA（基于扩散的分层控制架构），用于多AUV自组网在声学通信受限、动态拓扑和海洋扰动下的协同机动目标跟踪。实验表明该方法在收敛速度、跟踪精度和训练稳定性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多AUV自组网协同目标跟踪面临高维联合状态-动作建模和噪声敏感策略生成的问题，现有MARL方法训练不稳定、跟踪性能下降，亟需更鲁棒的去中心化协调方案。

Method: 1）构建多AUV协同跟踪的MDP建模，包含声呐探测机制和海洋流扰动；2）提出MDCA三层层级闭环控制框架（全局智能控制层、本地在线训练层、物理动作执行层），实现任务分配、局部决策与执行反馈的协同优化；3）提出VGG-MADiffRL算法，基于扩散策略并在逆向去噪过程中引入值梯度引导动作生成，采用孪生值网络联合优化和软目标更新以缓解过估计和训练振荡。

Result: 实验表明VGG-MADiffRL在协同跟踪场景中实现了更快的收敛速度、更高的跟踪精度和更平滑的训练动态，验证了其在动态水下环境中的有效性和工程价值。

Conclusion: 值梯度引导的扩散强化学习与分层控制架构的结合能有效解决多AUV协同跟踪中的高维建模和噪声敏感问题，在动态水下环境中具有实际工程应用潜力。

Abstract: Multi-AUV ad-hoc network-based target tracking requires networked autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to cooperatively track maneuvering targets under constrained acoustic communication, dynamic topology, and uncertain ocean disturbances. Although multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables decentralized coordination through centralized training, existing methods suffer from high-dimensional joint state-action modeling, noise-sensitive policy generation, leading to unstable training and degraded tracking. To address these issues, we propose VGG-MADiffRL, a value-gradient-guided multi-agent diffusion RL algorithm, and MDCA, a diffusion?based hierarchical control architecture. Leveraging underwater mission characteristics, we model sonar detection mechanisms and ocean current disturbances, formulating cooperative tracking for multi-AUV ad-hoc networks as an MDP. The proposed MDCA constitutes a three-tier closed-loop control framework: a global intelligent control layer, a local online training layer, and a physical action execution layer. This structure enables synergistic optimization across task allocation, local decision processes, and execution feedback. Within MDCA, the local online training layer is the policy learning framework; VGG-MADiffRL builds on diffusion policies and incorporates value gradients to guide action generation in the reverse denoising process, steering the generated actions towards higher expected returns. It employs twin value networks with joint optimization and soft target updates to mitigate overestimation and training oscillations, promoting more stable convergence. Experimental results show that VGG-MADiffRL consistently achieves faster convergence, higher tracking accuracy, and smoother training dynamics in cooperative tracking scenarios, validating its effectiveness and practical engineering value in dynamic underwater settings.

</details>


### [96] [Unifying Generative Models with Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12438)
*Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将生成建模统一表述为路径积分形式，其中流模型、扩散模型、变分模型和对抗模型均是该主作用量下不同的评估原则。通过MSRJD形式将自由概率流与相互作用概率流分离，开启图论微扰论，给出确定性采样器的一圈修正，并在可解及非线性漂移上验证（将53%的树级误差降至1.6%）。不完善的分数函数作为插入项导出响应加权的分数匹配目标，对称等变漂移设计成为具有EFT幂次计数的算子展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（流模型、扩散模型、变分模型、对抗模型）虽形式多样但缺乏统一的理论框架，难以在统一的数学语言下分析其共性、差异及系统化改进。作者希望借路径积分和量子场论的工具为生成建模建立统一的原理性框架。

Method: 将生成建模表述为一个主作用量的路径积分，不同的生成模型（流、扩散、变分、对抗）对应不同的评估原则。利用MSRJD形式分离自由概率流与相互作用概率流，引入费曼图微扰论；对确定性采样器计算一圈修正，分数误差作为外部插入项处理，并给出响应加权的分数匹配目标；对称等变漂移设计通过算子展开和EFT幂次计数框架实现。

Result: 在可解漂移和非线性漂移实验上，所提出的一圈修正将树级误差从53%降至1.6%，且不增加随机采样成本。同时推导出响应加权的分数匹配目标，并建立了对称等变漂移设计的EFT幂次计数体系。

Conclusion: 路径积分与MSRJD形式为生成建模提供了统一且可微扰求解的理论框架，使得确定性采样可低成本获得量子（场论）修正，同时为分数学习和对称性设计提供了系统的理论指导。

Abstract: We formulate generative modeling as a path integral in which flow-based, diffusion-based, variational, and adversarial models arise as different evaluation principles for a single master action. Its Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de~Dominicis (MSRJD) form separates free from interacting probability flows and opens them to diagrammatic perturbation theory. The expansion yields a one-loop correction to deterministic samplers at no stochastic-sampling cost, which we validate on solvable and nonlinear drifts, where it reduces a 53 % tree-level error to 1.6 %. Imperfect learned scores enter as insertions and yield a response-weighted score-matching objective, and symmetry-equivariant drift design becomes an operator expansion with EFT power counting.

</details>


### [97] [Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution: Architecture-Aligned Post-Hoc Explainability for Recurrent Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12441)
*Iyad Assaad Nekka,Hamida Seba,Khaled Walid Hidouci,Karima Amrouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了X-AddGraph，一个针对动态图异常检测框架AddGraph的严格事后可解释性框架，通过双时空归因机制（DSTA）为已有的GCN+GRU检测器提供解释，同时完全不改变检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图中的深度学习异常检测器已达到较高准确率，但缺乏可解释性：当一条边被标记为异常时，分析者只能获得一个分数而不知道原因。在需要审计和信任的协作式、受监管的信息系统中，这种不透明性是不可接受的。AddGraph作为基础的GCN+GRU动态图边缘级异常检测框架，此前从未配备任何形式的可解释性。

Method: 构建X-AddGraph，基于双时空归因（DSTA）机制，包含三个组件，各自对应AddGraph的一个架构模块：(1) 基于梯度的相关性归因，作用于当前邻接结构（空间维度）；(2) 直接读取推理期间已计算好的上下文注意力权重（短期时间维度，零额外成本）；(3) 通过循环隐藏状态的梯度回滚（长期时间维度）。检测器本身被冻结，因此检测性能完全保留。

Result: 检测性能完全不变（Delta AUC = 0，经验验证到小数点后十位）。在UCI Message基准测试上，训练好的AddGraph基线达到每个快照平均AUC 0.8705，超过原始发表结果；X-AddGraph逐分复现的同时新增了解释。在四类边缘群体（高置信度真阳性、低置信度真阳性、假阳性、随机样本）上，长期归因识别的历史快照携带的反事实信号显著强于随机选择（0.127对0.074），这是任何空间方向不可见的解释器无法提供的。

Conclusion: X-AddGraph成功为AddGraph提供了严格事后（post-hoc）的可解释性，在不牺牲任何检测性能的前提下产生空间和时间的归因解释，且长期时间归因能捕捉到其他方法无法提供的反事实信号。代码已公开发布以保证完全可复现性。

Abstract: Deep learning detectors for anomalies in dynamic graphs have reached strong accuracy, yet they remain opaque: when an edge is flagged, the analyst receives a score but no reason. This opacity is untenable in the cooperative, regulated information systems where such detectors are deployed, where automated decisions must be auditable and trustworthy. We address this gap for AddGraph, the foundational GCN+GRU framework for edge-level anomaly detection in dynamic graphs, which to our knowledge has never been equipped with any form of explainability. We present a strictly post-hoc explainability framework, X-AddGraph, built on a Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution (DSTA) mechanism whose three components are each aligned with one of AddGraph's architectural modules: a gradient-based relevance attribution over the current adjacency structure (spatial), a direct reading of the contextual attention weights already computed during inference (short-term temporal, at zero additional cost), and a gradient rollback through the recurrent hidden states (long-term temporal). Because the detector is frozen, detection performance is preserved exactly (Delta AUC = 0, verified empirically to ten decimal places). On the UCI Message benchmark, our trained AddGraph baseline reaches an average per-snapshot AUC of 0.8705, exceeding the originally published result; X-AddGraph reproduces every score identically while adding explanations where none existed. Evaluated across four edge populations - confident true positives, low-confidence true positives, false positives, and random samples - the long-term attribution identifies historical snapshots carrying significantly more counterfactual signal than random selection (0.127 vs. 0.074), a capability that no spatially-blind explainer can provide. We release our implementation for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [98] [Personalized Scorer Modeling: A Learning-Based Framework for Deriving Robust Sleep Stage Labels from Multiple Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12446)
*Seyyed Ali Hoseini,Javad Baseri,Hamid Saadatfar,Edris Hoseini Gol,AmirHossein Eshghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于学习的睡眠分期标注图（LBH）构建方法，通过建模每位评分者的分期行为（使用混淆矩阵）来聚合多位专家的标注，从而生成更可靠的参考催眠图。在DOD-H和DOD-O数据集上，使用随机森林+EEG+EMG组合取得最佳性能，准确率分别为86.07%和86.04%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动睡眠分期研究通常使用单一参考催眠图进行模型评估，忽视了评分者间变异（inter-scorer variability）的存在。单一参考标注可能不够可靠，因此需要探索如何利用多评分者数据集从多位专家的集体行为中构建更可靠的参考标签。

Method: 使用公开的DOD-H和DOD-O数据集，提取EEG（C3-M2）和chin EMG信号的30维特征（共60维融合特征）。提出学习式催眠图（LBH）方法：通过机器学习模型为每位评分者推导混淆矩阵，经列归一化后估计每个真实睡眠分期在给定评分者标签下的概率，再跨评分者聚合概率以分配每个epoch的最终标签。使用随机森林、支持向量机和多层感知机三种分类器，在EEG-only和EEG+EMG两种设置下评估，并与数据集催眠图（DH）和最佳评分者催眠图（BSH）进行比较。

Result: LBH在所有设置下均一致优于DH和BSH。最佳结果采用随机森林分类器配合EEG+EMG特征：DOD-H上达到86.07%准确率、85.46%精确率、85.29% F1分数；DOD-O上达到86.04%准确率、85.21%精确率、84.70% F1分数。

Conclusion: 个性化评分者建模能够在不丢弃任何专家信息的前提下改进参考催眠图的构建，为多评分者睡眠分期数据集提供了一种更可靠的标签聚合方案。

Abstract: Sleep stage classification is important for the diagnosis and management of sleep disorders, yet most automatic staging studies evaluate models against a single reference hypnogram despite known inter-scorer variability. This study investigates whether multi-scored datasets can be used to construct more reliable reference labels from the collective behavior of multiple experts. We use the publicly available DOD-H and DOD-O datasets. EEG (C3-M2) and chin EMG signals were segmented into 30-s epochs, and 30 features were extracted from each modality, yielding 60 features for EEG+EMG. We propose a learning-based hypnogram (LBH) that models the stage-specific behavior of each scorer using confusion matrices derived from machine-learning models. After column normalization, these matrices estimate the probability of each true sleep stage given each scorer's label; probabilities are aggregated across scorers to assign the final label for each epoch. LBH was evaluated with random forest, support vector machine, and multilayer perceptron classifiers under EEG-only and EEG+EMG settings, and compared with the dataset hypnogram (DH) and best-scorer hypnogram (BSH). LBH consistently improved overall performance. The best results were obtained with random forest and EEG+EMG, reaching 86.07% accuracy, 85.46% precision, and 85.29% F1-score on DOD-H, and 86.04% accuracy, 85.21% precision, and 84.70% F1-score on DOD-O. These findings suggest that personalized scorer modeling can improve reference hypnogram construction without discarding information from individual experts.

</details>


### [99] [Geometric and Behavioral Stratification in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12447)
*Nelson Guda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现训练后的Transformer模型存在以"预测方向"（即当前预测token的unembedding方向）为内容定义的锚定基。以该锚为参照，残差流的几何与行为特性按与预测的接近程度呈分层结构，该规律在18个模型中普遍成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究指出Transformer会形成"特权基"（协调轴统计特性与残差流其余部分不同），但尚未明确这类基具体选择何种方向。理解这一方向的性质对于揭示高维计算如何与线性读出共存的机制至关重要。

Method: 以预测方向（模型当前预测token的unembedding方向）为锚，系统测量残差流变异沿该方向的几何分层结构；对比方差基分析与锚定分析的重构能力；通过干扰实验（分层扰动不同深度的方差方向）验证因果作用，并跨18个模型（稠密/MoE，7B-120B，基础/指令微调）验证普适性。

Result: 预测方向充当内容定义的"特权锚"，残差流按距离预测方向的接近程度呈陡峭几何梯度：预测近端区域高度结构化且聚类相关提示，远端区域平坦且对提示组反判别。窄而尺度不变的预测接口集中了读出相关结构，方差基分析只能部分恢复该组织且缺口随提示异质性增大。干扰近端方向导致立即发散和任务帧切换，远端则延迟发散但保留帧。

Conclusion: 预测方向是与先前描述的坐标轴截然不同的特权锚，揭示了高维计算如何与线性读出共存的几何机制——窄的读出接口与庞大的负载互补区域协同工作，行为由方向而非幅度驱动。

Abstract: Trained transformer models develop privileged bases: coordinate axes whose statistics differ from the rest of the residual stream. But what kind of direction does such a basis select? We investigate the prediction direction, the unembedding direction of the token a model currently predicts, and find that it functions as a content-defined privileged anchor. Measured with respect to this anchor, residual-stream variation is geometrically and behaviorally stratified by proximity to the prediction.
  The stratification holds in all eighteen models tested (dense and mixture-of-experts, 7B-120B, base and instruction-tuned). A narrow, scale-invariant prediction interface concentrates readout-relevant structure, while the vast prediction-distal complement expands with model scale. Because the prediction direction sits nearly orthogonal to the principal variance axes, variance-based analyses recover this organization only partly, and the shortfall grows with prompt heterogeneity.
  Anchoring reveals a steep geometric gradient: prediction-proximal regions are highly structured and cluster related prompts, while the complement is flatter and anti-discriminates among prompt groups. The interface is a narrow slice but functionally decisive. Disrupting the variance directions closest to the prediction causes immediate divergence and frequent task-frame shifts; disrupting the next level down delays divergence and preserves framing. The complement is weakly readout-aligned per direction yet causally and temporally load-bearing, and behavior is driven by direction rather than magnitude.
  These results establish the prediction direction as a privileged anchor distinct from previously described coordinate axes, and give a geometric account of how high-dimensional computation coexists with linear readout.

</details>


### [100] [Exemplar-based objective classification of gust-induced loads across multiple flight conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12448)
*Paolo Olivucci,Kowshik Srivatsan,David E. Rival*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的客观分类方法，通过对大量实验观测数据进行编码和摘要化处理，选取少量高显著性的范例作为分类标准，用于组织复杂的气动阵风载荷。在3480个压力载荷测量数据库（涵盖六种飞行姿态的飞翼模型）上验证，发现了九种跨姿态重复出现的基本响应类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于粗参数（如飞行姿态）的标注方式虽然可解释，但难以客观组织多种飞行条件下阵风诱导载荷的复杂性。研究者希望找到一种既客观、又保持可解释性的分类准则。

Method: 方法包括两个核心步骤：(1) 通过机器学习表征编码大量实验观测数据；(2) 应用摘要化（summarization）程序选择最小子集的高显著性范例，作为基于相似性的客观分类准则。示范数据库为3480个随机阵风诱导的压力载荷测量，覆盖六种飞行姿态的飞翼模型。

Result: 研究发现九种基本响应类型在不同姿态间重复出现，且对每种类型的瞬态响应分析能够提供底层流体力学机制的物理直觉。

Conclusion: 基于范例的客观分类方法既能有效组织阵风载荷的复杂性，又保持了可解释性，便于专家检查和开展更精细的实验研究。

Abstract: Is it possible to find an objective classification criterion that organizes the complexity of gust-induced loads across many flight conditions? And one that remains as interpretable as a labelling based on coarse parameters, such as the flight attitude? Our approach encodes a large number of experimental observations through a machine-learned representation and applies a summarization procedure to select a minimal subset of highly significant exemplars. The exemplars provide a similarity-based objective classification criterion of all the observations, they can be more conveniently inspected by experts and can become subject of more refined experiments. We demonstrate the approach on a database of 3480 pressure-load measurements induced by random gusts on a flying-wing model across six flight attitudes. We find nine fundamental response types that recur across multiple attitudes; analysis of a type's transient response enables physical intuition into the underlying fluid mechanics.

</details>


### [101] [When Can You Trust Offline Evaluation of Equal-Cost Top-k Allocation? A Controlled, Reproducible Benchmark and Practitioner's Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12489)
*Binshuang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对离线策略评估（OPE）中的确定性top-k目标规则进行了系统性基准测试，覆盖六个估计器、五个数据集和两组已知效应扫描，并通过非模拟配对参考验证机制。研究发现三个核心问题：弱重叠主要由记录器-目标动作对齐决定而非记录器锐度；优化器诅咒无法通过交叉拟合结果 nuisance 解决，需要诚实的策略级分割（改变估计目标）；倾向性估计误差是最大的性能退化来源，会严重损害IPS但几乎不影响双重稳健估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在预算约束下决定对谁进行干预，并希望在部署目标规则前知道其效果。离线策略评估（OPE）可以从历史日志数据中估计策略价值，但可部署的确定性top-k策略消除了所有动作上的平均化，导致弱重叠问题直接影响估计，因此需要系统性地评估不同估计器在此场景下的表现。

Method: 对六个估计器（包括IPS、双重稳健估计及两个调整后的混合方法）在五个数据集和两组已知效应扫描上进行基准测试，并使用非模拟的配对参考验证机制。实验设计涵盖：分析记录器-目标动作对齐对重叠的影响；检验交叉拟合对优化器诅咒的效果；评估倾向性估计误差的影响。日志为合成数据，倾向性下限设为0.02以确保所有权重有界。

Result: (1) 弱重叠由记录器与目标动作的对齐程度决定，而非记录器锐度；有效样本量能排序日志环境的风险，但无法在单个日志中排序候选，且截止点不可迁移。(2) 交叉拟合结果 nuisance 无法修复优化器诅咒，反而使复用偏差更严重；诚实的策略级分割通过改变估计目标来避免复用。(3) 倾向性估计误差是最大的退化来源：OOF估计对IPS的损害超过任何其他压力测试，对DR影响很小，甚至能反转重叠诊断。

Conclusion: 离线策略评估中的确定性top-k策略存在三类关键风险：弱重叠风险由动作对齐主导、优化器诅咒无法通过nuisance交叉拟合解决、倾向性估计误差是最主要的退化因素。基准代码已公开（仅公开数据），所有精确值表面均为合成或半合成数据。

Abstract: Organizations decide whom to treat under a budget and want to know what a targeting rule would have earned before deploying it. Off-policy evaluation promises this from logged data, but the deployable rule is a deterministic top-k policy: it removes all averaging over actions, so weak overlap hits the estimate directly. We benchmark six estimators across five datasets and two known-effect sweeps, and validate the mechanisms against a non-simulated paired reference. First, weak overlap is governed by logger-target action alignment, not by logging sharpness alone: what governs support is the logger's probability of the target's actions. Sharpening a logger built from the target's own score barely moves overlap over the tested range; action-level disagreement collapses it. Effective sample size ranks this risk across logging environments, but is weak at ranking candidates within the single log a practitioner holds, and its cut point does not transfer. Second, the optimizer's curse is not fixed by cross-fitting the outcome nuisance. When the rule is fit on the data used to evaluate it, cross-fitting the nuisance alone leaves the reuse bias in place and makes it worse. Honest policy-level splitting avoids the reuse by targeting the learning procedure's value -- a change of estimand, not a de-biasing of the full-sample policy. Third, propensity-estimation error is the largest degradation we measure: an out-of-fold estimate hurts IPS more than any other stress we apply, leaves doubly-robust estimation almost unchanged, and can invert the overlap diagnostic itself. Logging is synthesized and propensities floored at 0.02, so every failure occurs with bounded weights; the floor also reduces the two tuned hybrids to their untuned parents, leaving four practically distinct estimators, and all exact-value surfaces are synthetic or semi-synthetic. We release the benchmark; public data only.

</details>


### [102] [Learning Under Treatment-Induced Label Indeterminacy with Expert Annotations of Counterfactual Outcomes: A Case Study in Neurological Prognostication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12477)
*Xiaobin Shen,Chloe Y. H. Huang,Jonathan Elmer,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评估临床预测模型的框架，该框架显式区分"确定病例"（结果可观测）和"不确定病例"（结果因治疗决策而不可观测，由专家给出反事实猜测）。研究发现，标准评估指标（如AUROC）无法揭示模型在不确定病例上的失败模式，且模型在两类病例上的性能存在显式权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测模型通常假设所有患者的结果都被清晰观测，但治疗决策可能使某些患者的临床相关结果永久不可观测（如心脏骤停后神经预后评估中的治疗决策导致结局不确定）。标准评估指标在这种场景下会掩盖模型在真正需要预后支持的患者群体上的重要失败模式。

Method: 使用2,497名心脏骤停后患者队列（其中1,429名结局因治疗决策而不可确定），由独立临床专家对不确定病例提供反事实结果猜测作为目标标签。提出一个评估框架，将评估显式拆分为确定病例和不确定病例；并提出一个简单预测模型，可利用两类病例的目标标签并在两者之间进行权衡。

Result: 在所提出的神经模型和表格基线中，相似确定病例AUROC的模型在确定病例Brier分数和不确定病例概率估计上存在显著差异。改善对不确定病例目标标签的对齐通常以确定病例准确率下降为代价，表明标准评估隐藏了这种显式权衡。

Conclusion: 当治疗决策决定临床有意义的结果是否可观测时，传统评估指标可能遗漏那些最需要预后支持的患者的失败模式。因此，预测模型的评估应有意识地拆分确定与不确定病例，以捕捉标准评估无法揭示的性能差异。

Abstract: Clinical prediction models are often developed as if the outcome of interest were cleanly observed for every patient. This assumption fails when treatment decisions make the clinically relevant outcome permanently unobservable. As a case study of this problem, we consider post-cardiac-arrest neurological prognostication using a cohort of 2,497 patients, including 1,429 patients whose outcomes were rendered indeterminate by treatment decisions. These patients with indeterminate outcomes were reviewed by independent clinical experts, who provided their guesses of counterfactual outcomes about what would have happened to the patients. We refer to these patients as uncertain cases. We also have patients for whom we observe their clinically relevant outcomes; we refer to these patients as certain cases. We propose a framework for evaluating prediction models that explicitly splits the evaluation between certain and uncertain cases. Here, we cannot easily evaluate both types of cases in a uniform manner as the available target labels differ. We then propose a simple prediction model that uses target labels from both certain and uncertain cases in a manner that allows us to trade off between them. Across the proposed neural model and a collection of tabular baselines, models with similar certain-case AUROC can nevertheless differ substantially in both certain-case Brier score and their probability estimates for uncertain cases. Improving alignment with target labels of uncertain cases for our proposed model generally comes at the cost of worse accuracy on certain cases, highlighting an explicit tradeoff that standard evaluation conceals. These results show that when treatment decisions determine whether clinically meaningful outcomes remain observable, conventional evaluation metrics can miss important failure modes in the very patients for whom prognostic support matters most.

</details>


### [103] [Interpretable Causal Discovery via Causal-Effect Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12640)
*Cixuan Zhang,Guy Van den Broeck,Benjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于罕见事件估计技术的贝叶斯因果发现方法，用于在给定因果效应等约束事件条件下推断因果图和参数的联合后验分布，从而支持条件性因果发现的解释性任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现的目标不仅是预测因果边，更重要的是能够解释和说明观测到的或假设的现象（如特别大的因果效应）。然而，现有贝叶斯因果发现方法在处理后验质量较小的条件事件时面临计算困难，因此需要一种能够高效处理这类条件推断任务的算法。

Method: 将条件因果发现建模为贝叶斯推断问题，目标是在给定事件（如因果效应约束）条件下推断因果图和参数的联合后验。借鉴罕见事件估计技术，通过逐步将粒子群推向约束区域，同时保持近似条件后验的样本，从而在联合图-参数空间中进行有效推断。

Result: 在合成图上对小规模和大规模场景进行了实验验证，证明了方法的准确性；并在Sachs蛋白数据集上的案例研究中表明，该方法能够通过提供通路级摘要来辅助科学探索。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法有效解决了条件贝叶斯因果发现中罕见事件导致的计算难题，不仅能准确推断条件后验，还能在实际生物数据中提供有意义的通路级解释，为科学探索提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to uncover the underlying causal relationships given data generated from a system. The goal, however, is not merely to predict causal edges given data, but also to be able to interpret and explain either observed or hypothesized phenomena, such as a particularly large causal effect. We consider this task of conditional causal discovery and cast it as a Bayesian inference problem, in which we target the posterior over causal graphs and parameters conditional on an event such as a causal-effect constraint. Unfortunately, this poses a computational challenge: existing approaches to Bayesian causal discovery struggle when the event has small posterior mass. To address this, we adapt rare-event estimation techniques to perform inference the joint graph-parameter space. Our method gradually drives a particle population toward the constrained region while maintaining samples that approximate the conditional posterior. Empirical evaluation on synthetic graphs validates the accuracy of our approach at small and large scales, and we show in a case study on the Sachs protein dataset how our method can be used to aid scientific exploration by providing pathway-level summaries.

</details>


### [104] [Training Under Challenge: Executable Certificates and Challenge-Closed Optimality for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12655)
*Farhang Yeganegi,Arian Eamaz,Mojtaba Soltanalian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Training Under Challenge"（挑战训练）框架，一种可执行证书（executable-certificate）机制，通过预先声明、架构有效的过程构建同类的备选网络并重新评估目标函数，以量化神经网络检查点的经验全局最优性差距（global-optimality gap）。该框架区分了平坦训练曲线背后多种可能的失败模式（全局最优、局部陷阱、表示受限、训练器不匹配），并针对平方损失提供了可检查的覆盖率条件与残差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦的训练曲线无法区分神经网络是否达到全局最优、陷入局部陷阱、表示能力受限或与训练器不匹配等不同状态。现有方法缺乏一种系统性的、可执行的机制来量化检查点与全局最优之间的差距，也无法提供可验证的证书来证明或否定最优性。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供一种能够产生可复现证据（replayable witness）的框架。

Method: （1）定义"Training Under Challenge"框架：预声明架构合法的过程构建相同认证类别中的完整替代网络，重新评估相同目标函数；任何更低值的候选作为可复现证人，给出检查点经验全局最优性差距的下界。（2）引入资源索引的挑战力模数（resource-indexed challenge-power modulus），刻画通过测试所允许的最大差距。（3）针对平方损失，证明块下降算子（block-decrease operators）使覆盖率可检查，并给出一致界和实现残差界。（4）证明逆向前沿（converse frontier）：在无覆盖率条件下，一阶ReLU训练器可收敛到无限多个精确条件头最优，却收敛到非全局点。（5）提出配对预测证书（paired predictive certificates）区分解码器未充分利用与表示不足。

Result: 在通道门控（channel-gated）ResNet-18蒸馏问题中（已知最优解），8个内部挑战覆盖了全部240个审计输出方向，实现残差界在真实差距的1.74×到3.02×范围内。量化去噪实验展示了诊断、修复和当前状态重新认证的完整流程。配对预测证书成功区分了解码器利用不足与表示能力不足两类问题。

Conclusion: 挑战训练框架提供了一种可执行、可复现的机制来系统评估神经网络的全局最优性差距。通过有限的测试套件的通过仅相对于该套件成立，全局差距结论需要单独的覆盖率机制加以保证。该框架不仅能够诊断多种失败模式，还能在修复后重新认证，为深度学习训练过程提供了理论上有依据、实践上可操作的验证工具。

Abstract: A flat training curve does not reveal whether a neural network has reached a global optimum, is locally trapped, is representation-limited, or is mismatched to its trainer. We introduce Training Under Challenge, an executable-certificate framework in which predeclared, architecture-valid procedures construct complete alternatives in the same certified class and reevaluate the same objective. Any lower-valued candidate is a replayable witness that lower-bounds the checkpoint's empirical global-optimality gap. Passing a finite suite is only suite-relative; global-gap conclusions require a separately justified coverage mechanism. We define a resource-indexed challenge-power modulus that characterizes the largest gap compatible with passage. For squared loss, current block-decrease operators make coverage checkable and yield uniform and realized-residual bounds. We prove the converse frontier: without coverage, a first-order ReLU trainer can reach infinitely many exact conditional head optima while converging to a non-global point. On a channel-gated ResNet-18 distillation problem with known optimum, eight internal challenges cover all 240 audited output directions, and realized-residual bounds lie within factors of 1.74--3.02 of the true gap. Paired predictive certificates separate decoder under-use from representation insufficiency, while quantized-denoising studies demonstrate diagnosis, repair, and current-state recertification.

</details>


### [105] [Exploring Oversmoothing with Householder Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12514)
*Bhaskar Karol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HouseGNN（Householder图神经网络），通过使用Householder反射器结合GroupSort构建逐段正交的图神经网络层，解决深层GNN中的过平滑问题，保证每一层节点欧氏范数得以保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深层图神经网络面临过平滑问题：随着网络深度增加，重复应用归一化图传播算子会导致节点表示逐渐向低信息子空间坍缩，损失判别性信息。标准GCN直接对隐藏表示反复施加传播操作，加速了这一退化过程。

Method: HouseGNN不同于标准GCN直接更新隐藏状态，而是仅利用聚合的邻域消息来估计一个反射方向；随后通过Householder反射器结合GroupSort激活函数更新节点嵌入，形成逐段正交的层。作者证明了三个核心性质：(i)每个内部层保持逐节点的欧氏范数；(ii)Householder反射器对消息具有尺度和符号不变性；(iii)节点间的成对距离可通过逐节点正交算子的不匹配而改变。

Result: 论文从理论上证明了HouseGNN三个核心性质：节点范数保持、消息的尺度/符号不变性，以及通过正交算子间不一致性实现节点间距离变化的能力，从而从机制上避免过平滑导致的表征坍缩。

Conclusion: HouseGNN通过Householder反射与GroupSort构造的正交层设计，在深层网络中保持节点表示的欧氏范数，从理论上验证了其能有效缓解过平滑问题，为深层GNN架构提供了新的正交化设计思路。

Abstract: Deep graph neural networks(GNNs) suffer from oversmoothing- a progressive collapse of node representation towards a low information subspace as network depth increases because the normalized graph propagation operator is repeatedly applied directly to the hidden representations. In this work we study Householder Graph Neural Network (HouseGNN). Rather than updating the hidden state like standard GCN, HouseGNN uses the aggregated neighbourhood message solely to estimate a reflection direction; the node embedding is then updated by a Householder reflector followed by GroupSort, yielding a piecewise orthogonal layer that preserves Euclidean norm at every node and at every depth. We prove three core properties: (i) every internal layer preserves the node-wise Euclidean norm; (ii) the Householder reflector is scale scale and sign-invariant in the message; and (iii) pairwise distance between nodes can change through mismatch between node-wise orthogonal operators.

</details>


### [106] [GENADA: efficient generative time series adversarial attack framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12535)
*Michael Baronov,Denis Vorobev,Margarita Rusanova,Petr Sokerin,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GENADA，一种基于生成式模型的对抗攻击方法，通过学习生成模型在单次前向传播中直接产生对抗扰动，避免了传统梯度迭代攻击中反复反向传播的高计算开销，在时间序列领域多个神经网络模型和数据集上验证了与强基线相当的攻击质量且推理耗时更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在时间序列分析任务中易受对抗攻击影响，而现有基于梯度的迭代攻击方法（如一阶优化方法）需要多次反向传播计算输入梯度，计算代价高昂，亟需一种更高效的对抗扰动生成方法。

Method: 提出了GENADA（生成式对抗攻击）框架，通过学习一个生成模型直接在单次前向传播中产生欺骗性扰动，并设计了相应的训练流程。方法包含单步和迭代两种生成攻击变体。

Result: 在多个时间序列领域的神经网络模型和数据集上进行验证（包括受控低维设定），实验结果表明GENADA能达到与强基线相当的攻击质量，同时生成扰动所需的推理时间更少。

Conclusion: GENADA证明了生成式方法可以成为传统迭代梯度攻击的高效替代方案，在大幅降低攻击推理开销的同时保持攻击有效性，为时间序列模型的安全性评估提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Deep learning models are widely used for time series analysis in domains such as healthcare, finance, energy systems, and environmental monitoring. However, these models remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks, where small input perturbations cause severe degradation in predictive performance. Commonly used gradient-based attacks, iterative first-order methods, are computationally burdensome, as they repeatedly backpropagate through the victim model to compute input gradients during a number of iterative refinement steps. We propose a GENerative ADversarial Attack (GENADA) that learns a generative model to produce deceptive perturbations directly in a single forward pass and a procedure to train it. Variants include single-step and iterative generative attack schemes. The validation considers attacks on several neural models and datasets in the time-series domain, a controlled, low-dimensional setting. Empirically, GENADA achieves comparable attack quality to strong baselines while requiring less time to generate perturbations during inference.

</details>


### [107] [CardioState-JEPA: Delay-Aware Cross-Modal Learning of a Shared Cardiac Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12944)
*Hamza Shafiq,Hung Manh Pham,Bin Zhu,Pan Zhou,Jun Hu,Aaqib Saeed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CardioState-JEPA，一个基于生理感知联合嵌入预测架构的心血管基础模型，在ECG、PPG和PCG三种模态上学习统一的共享表示，通过跨模态延迟对齐和两阶段训练策略，在25个下游任务上取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECG、PPG和PCG提供同一心动周期的互补视角，但现有心血管基础模型仅针对单一模态训练，未能利用跨传感器共享的生理学信息；且同步多传感器数据稀缺，阻碍了多模态联合建模。

Method: 采用生理感知的联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)：将异构波形映射到统一的token空间，用单一共享Transformer编码器处理，通过预测被遮蔽的潜在心脏状态进行预训练（将预训练目标置于共享生理而非传感器特定的波形外观）。提出学习的延迟对齐器(learned delay aligner)处理电学、机械和血流动力学事件之间的时间偏移；采用两阶段训练：先从丰富的单模态数据学习模态内结构，再用配对数据在潜在心脏时间中对齐模态。

Result: 作为冻结编码器在25个下游任务（覆盖ECG、PPG、PCG）上评估，相比最佳自监督信号基线：PPG分类平均提升8.2 AUROC点，PCG杂音检测提升18.8 AUROC点，ECG分类提升15.5 AUROC点；在若干ECG基准上达到或超过使用特权临床文本或监督标签训练的心血管模型。

Conclusion: 异构心血管信号可以相互监督，从而训练一个统一的基础模型来表征心脏生理学，打破了单一模态训练的限制，验证了跨模态共享表示学习的可行性。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG), photoplethysmography (PPG), and phonocardiography (PCG) provide complementary views of the same cardiac cycle, yet existing cardiac foundation models are trained for a single sensing modality, leaving the shared physiology across sensors unexploited. We introduce CardioState-JEPA, a cardiac foundation model to learn a single shared representation jointly across ECG, PPG, and PCG, built on a physiology-aware joint-embedding predictive architecture. The model maps heterogeneous waveforms into a common token space, processes them with a single shared Transformer encoder, and learns by predicting masked latent cardiac states, placing the pretraining target on shared physiology rather than sensor-specific waveform appearance. To handle the temporal offsets between electrical, mechanical, and hemodynamic events, cross-modal prediction uses a learned delay aligner that matches signals at the corresponding cardiac time. Because synchronized multi-sensor recordings are scarce, CardioState-JEPA first learns within-modality structure from abundant unimodal data and then uses paired data to align modalities in latent cardiac time. Evaluated as a frozen encoder across 25 downstream tasks spanning ECG, PPG, and PCG, our encoder improves average PPG classification by 8.2 AUROC points, PCG murmur detection by 18.8 AUROC points, and ECG classification by 15.5 AUROC points over the best self-supervised signal baseline and matches or exceeds cardiac models trained with privileged clinical text or supervised labels on several ECG benchmarks. These results establish that heterogeneous cardiac signals can mutually supervise a single foundation model of cardiac physiology.

</details>


### [108] [Scaling Automatic Research Agents via World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12564)
*Xiyuan Yang,Sheikh Sarwar,Jingru Cheng,Zhan Shi,Duanshun Li,Huiyuan Chen,Haiyang Zhang,Chenlei Guo,Jingrui He,Zhenyu Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出世界模型强化学习（WMRL）方法，将AutoResearch智能体训练中的环境执行替换为世界模型推理，解决环境执行成为瓶颈的问题，并通过在线去偏和逆方差降噪两种技术缓解世界模型不完美带来的偏差和噪声，实现了3-4倍训练加速和更好的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化学术研究（AutoResearch）智能体是现代LLM的重要应用方向，但其后训练（尤其是强化学习）面临一个根本性矛盾：智能体生成与环境执行在规模化扩展方式上严重不匹配——所有生成过程可以共享批处理计算资源，而每个执行过程却独占沙盒和真实机器时间。这导致环境执行成为训练成本的主导因素和瓶颈。

Method: 提出World Model RL（WMRL）框架：1）将传统RL中的环境执行替换为世界模型推理，消除执行瓶颈；2）针对世界模型不完美（奖励含偏差和噪声）的问题，设计Online Debiasing（在线去偏）来抵消偏差，以及Inverse-Variance Denoising（逆方差降噪）来抑制噪声；3）从理论上证明两种缓解策略严格改善收敛保证。

Result: 在多种任务和不同智能体规模上，WMRL相比标准RL基线实现3-4倍训练加速且性能超越基线；后训练的4B和9B智能体在保留基准测试上超越48B和120B的开源权重智能体；WMRL还成功迁移到具身VLA策略的后训练，证明其泛化能力。

Conclusion: WMRL通过世界模型替代环境执行有效解决了AutoResearch智能体RL训练中的扩展性瓶颈，并提供了应对世界模型不完美性的理论支持和工程化方案，在性能、效率和泛化性上均展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Automating empirical research is a long-standing direction of AI. Recent automatic research (AutoResearch) agents bring this goal within reach, as modern LLMs show the capability to independently implement solutions and learn from the execution outcomes. Behind these gains, post-training (especially RL) plays a central role. In this paper, we identify a fundamental tension when scaling RL for these agents: the two components of every AutoResearch trajectory (agent generation and environment execution) scale in very different manners, since all generation shares compute through batching, while each execution occupies its exclusive sandbox and real machine time. As a result, the environment execution dominates the training cost and becomes the bottleneck as trajectories grow. To resolve this tension, we propose World Model RL (WMRL), which replaces environment execution with a world model to remove this bottleneck. Additionally, the world model can be imperfect, as its rewards are corrupted by bias and noise. Therefore, we further equip WMRL with two mitigations, Online Debiasing and Inverse-Variance Denoising, which offset the bias and suppress the noise respectively. Theoretically, we prove that both mitigations of WMRL strictly improve the convergence guarantee. Empirically, WMRL accelerates training by 3-4x on various tasks at different agent scales, while exceeding the performance of standard RL baselines. Moreover, our post-trained 4B and 9B agents outperform much larger open-weight agents of 48B and 120B on held-out benchmarks. Beyond AutoResearch, WMRL also transfers to post-training embodied VLA policies, which demonstrates the generalizability of our method.

</details>


### [109] [Prof-K: Probabilistic One-Pass Filtering for Efficient Top-k Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12573)
*Tadeusz Dziarmaga,Witold Sikora,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor,Marcin Mazur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Prof-K，一种快速、可扩展且分布无关的top-k选择算法，通过单遍过滤（随机采样估计自适应阈值 + 流式缓冲 + 精确top-k）实现概率正确性保证，在保持高概率正确性的同时实现1.5x-10x加速，并应用于稀疏自编码器训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据规模增长，现有top-k方法效率低下：精确方法内存和计算开销高，近似方法依赖脆弱的启发式策略，在对抗性或重尾输入下性能退化。需要一种既快速又可扩展、且对输入分布具有鲁棒性的top-k算法。

Method: Prof-K采用单遍过滤流程：(1) 用小的随机样本估计自适应阈值；(2) 将N个输入元素一次性流式传输到紧凑缓冲区；(3) 在缓冲区上运行精确top-k例程。对正确性和缓冲区大小推导高概率保证，并给出近似最优的样本大小以最小化N和k函数的开销。

Result: 相比高度优化的PyTorch topk和RadiK，Prof-K实现1.5x-10x加速，在大型数据集和小到中等k值场景增益最大。正确性保证不依赖于输入分布，对对抗性设置鲁棒。通过放宽召回目标（如恢复95%真实top-k），还可提供精确度-速度权衡。在BatchTopK稀疏自编码器训练中验证了实际效果。

Conclusion: Prof-K提供了一种分布无关、具有概率正确性保证的快速top-k算法，兼顾了精确性和效率，在训练成本中top-k占较大比例的SAE等场景中展示了显著的实用价值。

Abstract: Top-k selection is a fundamental computational primitive with applications spanning databases, information retrieval, signal processing, and modern machine learning workloads, including sparse activations and attention pruning. As data sizes grow, existing approaches become inefficient: exact methods incur high memory and compute overhead, while approximate methods often rely on brittle heuristics that degrade under adversarial or heavy-tailed inputs. In this paper, we introduce Prof-K, a fast, scalable, and distribution-agnostic top-k algorithm with probabilistic correctness guarantees. Prof-K performs a single-pass filtering procedure: a small random sample estimates an adaptive threshold, the N input elements are streamed once into a compact buffer, and an exact top-k routine on this buffer recovers the true top-k elements with probability at least 1 - $ε$, where $ε$ > 0 is user specified. We derive high-probability guarantees for correctness and buffer size, together with an approximately optimal sample size that minimizes overhead as a function of N and k. Empirically, Prof-K achieves 1.5x-10x speedups over the highly optimized PyTorch topk and recent RadiK implementations, with the largest gains in the large-scale, small-to-moderate-k regime where prior methods struggle most. Unlike previous approaches, these guarantees hold independently of the input distribution, ensuring robustness to adversarial settings. By relaxing the recall target (e.g., recovering 95% of the true top-k values), Prof-K additionally provides a principled accuracy-speed trade-off. We further demonstrate its impact on training BatchTopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), where top-k selection constitutes a significant portion of the training cost.

</details>


### [110] [The data geometry of masking diffusion: Certified-optimal schedules via unmasking growth complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13520)
*Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究离散采样的掩蔽扩散（masking diffusion），提出了一种称为"去掩蔽生长复杂度"（UGC）的路径解析数据几何度量。UGC的局部增量直接控制KL离散化误差，统一了伯努利子集和固定基数去掩蔽方案的分析。在log-reveal-odds坐标下，该结构可优化单块和多块调度，并量化自适应计算投入带来的收益。本文还展示了如何通过耦合揭示轨迹上的KL增量从样本中估计UGC增量，从而构建认证最优采样器，其迭代复杂度在常数因子内逼近对应的预言机过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型的采样效率高度依赖于去掩蔽调度（unmasking schedule）的设计，但现有分析缺乏一个统一的路径级框架来量化数据几何对离散化误差的影响，也缺少从数据中自适应调度的理论保证。

Method: 提出UGC（Unmasking Growth Complexity）作为路径级数据几何度量；其增量直接绑定KL离散化误差，在log-reveal-odds坐标下推导单块/多块最优调度；利用耦合揭示轨迹上的KL增量估计UGC增量，构建满足预设KL误差且迭代复杂度在常数因子内的认证最优采样器；并分析细划分极限下的Euler离散化误差渐近行为。

Result: 在细划分极限下，平方根UGC密度的平方积分决定了尖锐的首阶最优Euler离散化误差。示例表明相对于粗糙调度可获得显著的维度相关增益，包括通过常数个自适应放置的块即可实现Ω̃(√d)的改进。

Conclusion: UGC提供了一个统一的、可估计的路径级框架来分析和优化离散扩散的采样调度，兼具理论最优性保证和实践中的维度增益，并对细划分极限下的最优离散化误差给出了尖锐刻画。

Abstract: We study masking diffusion for discrete sampling and introduce a path-resolved measure of data geometry called the \emph{unmasking growth complexity} ({\textsf{UGC}\xspace}). Its local increments directly control Kullback--Leibler (KL) discretization error, yielding a unified analysis of Bernoulli-subset and fixed-cardinality unmasking schemes. In log-reveal-odds coordinates, this structure yields optimized single-block and multi-block schedules, and quantifies the gains from adapting computational effort to data geometry. Crucially, we show how {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} increments can be estimated from samples via KL increments along coupled reveal trajectories. This leads to \emph{certified-optimal} samplers that achieve a prescribed KL error with high probability and iteration complexity within a constant factor of the corresponding oracle procedure. Collapsing the \ugc path yields the aggregate {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} mass, which connects to classical multivariate dependence measures and complexity measures from previous analyses of discrete diffusion. In the fine-partition limit, the squared integral of the square-root {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} density determines the sharp leading-order optimal Euler discretization error. Examples exhibit substantial dimension-dependent gains over coarse schedules, including $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt{d})$ improvements achievable with a constant number of adaptively placed blocks.

</details>


### [111] [Represent, Then Generate: Multimodal-Conditioned Time-Series Generation under Irregular Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12592)
*Haochen Zhang,Jiaheng Guo,Yu-Chao Huang,Nicholas Knoz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCoGen，一个两阶段的生理时间序列条件生成框架，通过解耦多模态条件表征与目标生成，在医疗监测场景下合成缺失的侵入性生理信号（如连续血糖、动脉血压），在16个（数据集、任务、指标）设置上全部超越六种代表性基线生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床监测中许多高信息量的生理信号是侵入性、昂贵或对特定患者不可用的。条件生成可以从未缺失信号和临床变量中合成缺失信号，但现有生成器均基于单一条件模态构建，无法应对实际临床中异构、不规则缺失的时间变信号与静态协变量混合的场景。

Method: ReCoGen（Represent Conditions, then Generate）采用两阶段框架。阶段一为每种模态训练一个掩码自编码器（masked autoencoder），将每个时间变条件蒸馏为紧凑且容忍缺失的token序列；阶段二训练一个flow-matching生成器，将这些token与静态条件融合以合成目标信号。

Result: 在三个生理基准（AI-READI上的连续血糖监测、MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上的动脉血压生成）上，ReCoGen在所有16个（数据集、任务、指标）设置中获得最佳下游效用，超越了六种代表性条件生成器；其中13个设置的下游效用达到或超过真实信号上测量的效用。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明增益来自条件路径：跨可学习注意力处理冻结的逐模态编码器，以及针对静态条件的双token+AdaLN路径。ReCoGen将常规采集的信号转化为侵入性或不可用信号的信息性替代品，向着更少侵入、更低成本的连续临床监测迈进一步。

Abstract: Continuous physiological time series underpin modern clinical monitoring, yet many of the most informative signals are invasive, expensive, or simply unavailable for a given patient. Conditional generation offers a remedy: an absent signal can be synthesized from co-recorded signals and routine clinical variables. Existing generators, however, are built around a single conditioning modality and degrade when forced to handle the heterogeneous, irregularly missing mix of time-variant signals and static covariates seen in practice. We propose ReCoGen (Represent Conditions, then Generate), a two-stage framework that decouples multimodal condition representation from target generation. Stage I trains one masked autoencoder per modality, distilling each time-variant condition into a compact and missingness-tolerant token sequence. Stage II trains a flow-matching generator that fuses these tokens with static conditions to synthesize the target signal. Across three physiological benchmarks, including continuous glucose monitoring on AI-READI and arterial blood pressure generation on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, ReCoGen attains the best downstream utility on all sixteen (dataset, task, metric) settings, surpassing six representative conditional generators; on thirteen of them its utility also reaches or exceeds the utility measured on the real signal, a reference we read as an approximate anchor rather than a ceiling. Ablations trace the gains to the conditioning path: learnable cross-attention over the frozen per-modality encoders, and a dual token-plus-AdaLN route for the static conditions. ReCoGen thus turns routinely collected signals into informative surrogates for invasive or unavailable ones, a step toward less invasive, lower-cost continuous clinical monitoring.

</details>


### [112] [Exponential Convex Calibration Dimension for the Multi-Label Jaccard Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13549)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多标签分类和二元分割中逐实例Jaccard分数（IoU）的校准特性。作者证明了Jaccard分数、移位损失和普通损失矩阵的非奇异性质，并证明其损失列的仿射维度为2^s-1。通过精确校准分析，他们确立CCdim的下界和上界，表明零遗憾校准需要指数级预测维度，但任意固定可加遗憾容差都可以通过多项式维度实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jaccard分数（IoU）是多标签分类和二元分割中的标准评估指标，但其损失矩阵的数学性质和可校准性尚未被充分理解。核心问题在于：是否存在具有多项式预测维度的精确校准凸替代损失？这直接关系到实用算法的设计空间。

Method: 作者结合有限MinHash Gram表示与布尔Möbius反演来证明损失矩阵的非奇异性质；通过因式加权分布构造下界证明；提出F1到Jaccard的遗憾转移方法，以及MinHash平方损失替代方法，分别给出多项式维度的近似保证。

Result: 证明了Jaccard相关损失矩阵的非奇异性和仿射维度2^s-1；确立CCdim界限2^{s-1}≤CCdim≤2^s-1；给出F1到Jaccard的转移实现渐近Jaccard遗憾至多3-2√2；MinHash平方损失替代在任意条件标签分布上达到任意遗憾容差，直接构造维度O((s^2+s log(1/ρ))/α^2)，带符号变体维度O((s+log(1/ρ))/α^2)。

Conclusion: 零遗憾的精确校准需要指数级预测维度，这是一个根本性的下界；然而，任何固定的可加遗憾容差都可以通过多项式预测维度实现。这为实际应用提供了明确的设计准则：在有限遗憾容差下，可以利用多项式复杂度的替代损失方法。

Abstract: The per-instance Jaccard score, or intersection over union (IoU), is standard in multi-label classification and binary segmentation. With $s$ labels, its loss matrix has $2^s$ outcomes and reports. Under the convention $\mathrm{Jac}(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, we prove that the Jaccard score, shifted-loss, and ordinary loss matrices are nonsingular and that the loss columns have affine dimension $2^s-1$. The proof combines a finite MinHash Gram representation with Boolean Möbius inversion. For exact calibration, we prove $2^{s-1} \leq \mathrm{CCdim}(L^{\mathrm{Jac}}) \leq 2^s-1$. The lower bound uses a factorially weighted distribution with $2^{s-1}+1$ supported outcomes and Bayes-optimal reports. Consequently, every exactly calibrated convex surrogate requires exponentially many prediction coordinates. We also give two polynomial-dimensional approximation guarantees with explicit regret transfers. A new $F_1$-to-Jaccard transfer turns an existing $(s^2+1)$-dimensional $F_1$ surrogate into a polynomial-time rule with asymptotic Jaccard regret at most $3-2\sqrt{2}$. For any $α>0$ and $0<ρ<1$, a MinHash square-loss surrogate attains Jaccard-regret floor $α$ uniformly over arbitrary conditional label distributions. With probability at least $1-ρ$, the direct construction has dimension $O((s^2+s\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$, while a signed variant has dimension $O((s+\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$. Thus zero-regret calibration requires exponential dimension, whereas every fixed additive regret tolerance admits polynomial prediction dimension.

</details>


### [113] [Predicting When Random Low-Dimensional Reparameterizations Train Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12597)
*Andrew Cheng,Ali Eslamian,Jie Cheng,Mehdi Zargham,Qiang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Neural networks can often be trained or fine-tuned through random low-dimensional reparameterization, where a small latent vector is mapped into a full parameter update by a frozen random map. This raises a practical question: how large must the latent search space be to reach a low-loss region? We first express the known accessibility transition in an equivalent conic form, centered for compact convex targets at the statistical dimension of the polar cone. Our main theoretical contribution is an orientation-resolved quadratic master formula that predicts the random-slice residual from both the curvature spectrum and the reference-to-solution displacement profile. It yields a self-consistent isotropic-orientation predictor and, in a conservative radius-only specialization, recovers the earlier Gaussian-width quadratic bound. Building on this analysis, we introduce Random Mapping Networks (RaMaN), which instantiate the predicted latent dimension using structured Hadamard or seed-regenerated Gaussian maps. These constructions avoid the O(dP) storage of dense random maps and reduce optimizer-state memory from O(P) to O(d). We also develop matrix-free curvature approximations and sweep-free dimension selection. Across controlled quadratic and neural-curvature experiments, the orientation-resolved predictor closely tracks measured transition locations and outperforms orientation-agnostic approximations when displacement direction matters. End-to-end experiments further show sharp, protocol-dependent training transitions across image and language models.

</details>


### [114] [Defensive Boosting for Online Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13554)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"防御性提升器"（Defensive Booster）算法，通过操作化提升的"对偶视角"，在自适应对手的在线概率预测中同时获得在线梯度提升的Brier分数保证和在线弱到强提升的分类错误保证，并且只需访问单个弱类学习器，在效率和性能上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线提升技术在二进制结果的在线概率预测中提供两种不兼容的保证：在线梯度提升在Brier分数上与H张成空间中最优预测器竞争，但当张成空间中不含精确预测器时无保证；在线弱到强提升在弱学习条件下驱动分类错误归零，但条件不满足时也无保证。需要一个统一框架同时获得这两种保证。

Method: 提出Defensive Booster算法，一种简单的防御性预测算法，通过操作化提升的"对偶视图"实现：当算法的随机化分类错误持续较高时，其错误权重形成一个平滑的重新加权分布，使每个弱假设在其上都具有低边（low edge），从而产生事后硬核证书证明弱学习条件不成立。同时开发了强适应变体，在每个时间区间上均满足两种保证。算法仅访问一个弱类学习器。

Result: Defensive Booster在每条自适应序列上以与在线梯度提升相同的速率获得与H张成空间中最佳预测的Brier分数竞争力；同时，每当实现记录满足平滑弱学习条件时，其Brier分数和随机化分类错误的速率保证与在线分类提升相同。合成和真实数据流实验表明其预测性能强（有时显著优于所有先前基线），且运行速度快数个数量级。

Conclusion: Defensive Booster首次在在线概率预测中同时实现Brier分数竞争性和弱到强提升两种保证，并且只需维持单个弱类学习器而非大型集成，显著提升了效率。强适应变体进一步在所有时间区间上保持两种保证，为在线提升理论提供了统一的优雅解决方案。

Abstract: We study online probabilistic forecasting of binary outcomes chosen by an adaptive adversary.
